"""World-model benchmark: tests WorldState, GoalTracker, and PlanningEngine.
RECONSTRUCTED 2026-08-30 from:
  - C:\RealAI-clean\benchmarks\base.py (BaseBenchmark / BenchmarkResult)
  - C:\RealAI-clean\benchmarks\bench_memory.py (bench pattern)
  - C:\RealAI-clean\realai\benchmarks\world_model.py (WorldState / GoalTracker / PlanningEngine API)
No original bench_world_model.py body was recovered from any tree.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any
from benchmarks.base import BaseBenchmark, BenchmarkResult
class WorldModelBenchmark(BaseBenchmark):
    """Tests world-model observe/plan/goal tracking."""
    name = "world_model"
    def run(self, model: Any = None) -> BenchmarkResult:
        """Exercise WorldState, GoalTracker, and PlanningEngine.
        Args:
            model: Unused.
        Returns:
            BenchmarkResult with three static API checks.
        """
        try:
            from realai.world_model import (
                GOAL_TRACKER,
                PLANNING_ENGINE,
                WORLD_STATE,
                GoalTracker,
                PlanningEngine,
                WorldState,
            )
        except ImportError:
            try:
                from realai.benchmarks.world_model import (
                    GOAL_TRACKER,
                    PLANNING_ENGINE,
                    WORLD_STATE,
                    GoalTracker,
                    PlanningEngine,
                    WorldState,
                )
            except ImportError:
                return BenchmarkResult(
                    name=self.name,
                    score=0.0,
                    total=1,
                    passed=0,
                    details=[{"error": "Could not import world_model"}],
                )
        passed = 0
        details = []
        total = 3
        state = WorldState()
        obs = state.observe(
            "The capital of France is Paris",
            confidence=1.0,
            source="bench",
        )
        state.set_fact("capital_france", "Paris", 1.0)
        fact_ok = state.get_fact("capital_france") == "Paris" and obs is not None
        if fact_ok:
            passed += 1
        details.append({"test": "world_state_fact", "passed": fact_ok})
        tracker = GoalTracker()
        goal = tracker.add_goal("Build a REST API", sub_goals=["Design", "Implement"])
        listed = tracker.list_goals()
        goal_ok = goal is not None and len(listed) >= 1
        if goal_ok:
            passed += 1
        details.append({
            "test": "goal_tracker_add",
            "passed": goal_ok,
            "count": len(listed),
        })
        engine = PlanningEngine()
        steps = engine.plan("Build a REST API", state, max_steps=3)
        plan_ok = isinstance(steps, list) and len(steps) >= 1
        if plan_ok:
            passed += 1
        details.append({
            "test": "planning_engine_plan",
            "passed": plan_ok,
            "steps": len(steps) if plan_ok else 0,
        })
        _ = (WORLD_STATE, GOAL_TRACKER, PLANNING_ENGINE)
        return BenchmarkResult(
            name=self.name,
            score=passed / total,
            total=total,
            passed=passed,
            details=details,
        )

"""World-model benchmark: tests WorldState, GoalTracker, and PlanningEngine.
RECONSTRUCTED 2026-08-30 from:
  - C:\\RealAI-clean\\benchmarks\\base.py (BaseBenchmark / BenchmarkResult)
  - C:\\RealAI-clean\\benchmarks\\bench_memory.py (bench pattern)
  - C:\\RealAI-clean\\realai\\benchmarks\\world_model.py (WorldState / GoalTracker / PlanningEngine API)
No original bench_world_model.py body was recovered from any tree.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any
from benchmarks.base import BaseBenchmark, BenchmarkResult
class WorldModelBenchmark(BaseBenchmark):
    """Tests world-model observe/plan/goal tracking."""
    name = "world_model"
    def run(self, model: Any = None) -> BenchmarkResult:
        try:
            from realai.world_model import (
                GOAL_TRACKER,
                PLANNING_ENGINE,
                WORLD_STATE,
                GoalTracker,
                PlanningEngine,
                WorldState,
            )
        except ImportError:
            try:
                from realai.benchmarks.world_model import (
                    GOAL_TRACKER,
                    PLANNING_ENGINE,
                    WORLD_STATE,
                    GoalTracker,
                    PlanningEngine,
                    WorldState,
                )
            except ImportError:
                return BenchmarkResult(
                    name=self.name,
                    score=0.0,
                    total=1,
                    passed=0,
                    details=[{"error": "Could not import world_model"}],
                )
        passed = 0
        details = []
        total = 3
        state = WorldState()
        obs = state.observe(
            "The capital of France is Paris",
            confidence=1.0,
            source="bench",
        )
        state.set_fact("capital_france", "Paris", 1.0)
        fact_ok = state.get_fact("capital_france") == "Paris" and obs is not None
        if fact_ok:
            passed += 1
        details.append({"test": "world_state_fact", "passed": fact_ok})
        tracker = GoalTracker()
        goal = tracker.add_goal("Build a REST API", sub_goals=["Design", "Implement"])
        listed = tracker.list_goals()
        goal_ok = goal is not None and len(listed) >= 1
        if goal_ok:
            passed += 1
        details.append({
            "test": "goal_tracker_add",
            "passed": goal_ok,
            "count": len(listed),
        })
        engine = PlanningEngine()
        steps = engine.plan("Build a REST API", state, max_steps=3)
        plan_ok = isinstance(steps, list) and len(steps) >= 1
        if plan_ok:
            passed += 1
        details.append({
            "test": "planning_engine_plan",
            "passed": plan_ok,
            "steps": len(steps) if plan_ok else 0,
        })
        _ = (WORLD_STATE, GOAL_TRACKER, PLANNING_ENGINE)
        return BenchmarkResult(
            name=self.name,
            score=passed / total,
            total=total,
            passed=passed,
            details=details,
        )
benchmarks/bench_stack_health.py (wraps stack_health.check; Grok’s bench_stack.py is unresolved):

"""Canonical filename: bench_stack_health.py
RECONSTRUCTED 2026-08-30. No original body existed.
Related: scanners/stack_health.py (static path presence).
Does not start servers, training, or dispatch.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
STATUS = "reconstructed_static"
CANONICAL_NAME = "bench_stack_health"
RECOVERED_FROM = "scanners/stack_health.py"
try:
    from benchmarks.base import BaseBenchmark, BenchmarkResult
except ImportError:
    BaseBenchmark = object  # type: ignore
    class BenchmarkResult:  # type: ignore
        def __init__(self, name: str, score: float, total: int, passed: int, details: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None) -> None:
            self.name = name
            self.score = score
            self.total = total
            self.passed = passed
            self.details = details or []
class StackHealthBenchmark(BaseBenchmark):
    name = "stack_health"
    def run(self, model: Any = None) -> BenchmarkResult:
        try:
            from scanners.stack_health import check
            report = check()
        except ImportError:
            try:
                from realai.scanners.stack_health import check  # type: ignore
                report = check()
            except ImportError:
                return BenchmarkResult(
                    name=self.name,
                    score=0.0,
                    total=1,
                    passed=0,
                    details=[{"error": "stack_health.check not importable", "executed_runtime": False}],
                )
        present = list(report.get("present") or [])
        missing = list(report.get("missing") or [])
        total = max(len(present) + len(missing), 1)
        passed = len(present)
        return BenchmarkResult(
            name=self.name,
            score=passed / total,
            total=total,
            passed=passed,
            details=[{"present": present, "missing": missing, "executed_runtime": False}],
        )

scanners/ability_matrix.py (loads realai/abilities/registry.json only):

"""Canonical filename: ability_matrix.py
RECONSTRUCTED 2026-08-30. No original body in any tree.
Related: realai/abilities/registry.json and scanners/ability_inventory.py.
Does not scan or execute abilities.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
STATUS = "reconstructed_static"
CANONICAL_NAME = "ability_matrix"
RECOVERED_FROM = "realai/abilities/registry.json"
_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_DEFAULT = _ROOT / "realai" / "abilities" / "registry.json"
def load_matrix(path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    target = path or _DEFAULT
    if not target.is_file():
        return {"status": "missing_artifact", "path": str(target)}
    try:
        data = json.loads(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return {"status": "loaded_artifact_only", "data": data}
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"status": "unreadable", "error": str(exc), "path": str(target)}
def describe() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    loaded = load_matrix()
    abilities = []
    data = loaded.get("data") or {}
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        abilities = data.get("abilities") or []
    return {
        "canonical": CANONICAL_NAME,
        "status": STATUS,
        "artifact_status": loaded.get("status"),
        "ability_count": len(abilities) if isinstance(abilities, list) else 0,
        "executed_runtime": False,
    }
plugins/plugin_registry.py (wraps builder shape; loads JSON, no disk hunt):

"""Canonical filename: plugin_registry.py
RECONSTRUCTED 2026-08-30 as a wrap of scripts/plugin_registry_builder.py.
Does NOT run find_plugins() / disk hunts / writes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
STATUS = "reconstructed_wrap"
CANONICAL_NAME = "plugin_registry"
RECOVERED_FROM = "scripts/plugin_registry_builder.py"
_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_DEFAULT = _ROOT / "realai" / "plugins" / "registry.json"
def load_registry(path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    target = path or _DEFAULT
    if not target.is_file():
        return {"status": "missing_artifact", "path": str(target), "plugins": []}
    try:
        data = json.loads(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"status": "unreadable", "error": str(exc), "path": str(target), "plugins": []}
    if isinstance(data, list):
        return {"status": "loaded", "plugins": data}
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        plugins = data.get("plugins") or data.get("abilities") or []
        return {"status": "loaded", "plugins": plugins, "raw_keys": list(data.keys())}
    return {"status": "unexpected_shape", "plugins": []}
def list_plugins() -> List[Any]:
    return list(load_registry().get("plugins") or [])
def describe() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    loaded = load_registry()
    return {
        "canonical": CANONICAL_NAME,
        "status": STATUS,
        "recovered_from": RECOVERED_FROM,
        "artifact_status": loaded.get("status"),
        "plugin_count": len(loaded.get("plugins") or []),
        "writes": False,
        "scans": False,
    }

Rewritten bench_world_model.py

python

""" World Model Benchmark Rewritten 2026-08-30 for RealAI static reconstruction.  Purpose:     Validate the core world-model APIs:         - WorldState.observe / set_fact / get_fact         - GoalTracker.add_goal / list_goals         - PlanningEngine.plan  This benchmark does not require a model and performs deterministic checks. """  from __future__ import annotations from typing import Any from benchmarks.base import BaseBenchmark, BenchmarkResult   def _import_world_model():     """Try both canonical RealAI import paths."""     try:         from realai.world_model import (             WorldState,             GoalTracker,             PlanningEngine,         )         return WorldState, GoalTracker, PlanningEngine     except ImportError:         try:             from realai.benchmarks.world_model import (                 WorldState,                 GoalTracker,                 PlanningEngine,             )             return WorldState, GoalTracker, PlanningEngine         except ImportError:             return None, None, None   class WorldModelBenchmark(BaseBenchmark):     name = "world_model"      def run(self, model: Any = None) -> BenchmarkResult:         WorldState, GoalTracker, PlanningEngine = _import_world_model()          if not WorldState:             return BenchmarkResult(                 name=self.name,                 score=0.0,                 total=1,                 passed=0,                 details=[{"error": "world_model import failed"}],             )          total = 3         passed = 0         details = []          # --- Test 1: WorldState fact handling ---         state = WorldState()         state.observe("The capital of France is Paris", confidence=1.0, source="bench")         state.set_fact("capital_france", "Paris", 1.0)         fact_ok = state.get_fact("capital_france") == "Paris"         passed += fact_ok         details.append({"test": "world_state_fact", "passed": fact_ok})          # --- Test 2: GoalTracker basic goal creation ---         tracker = GoalTracker()         goal = tracker.add_goal("Build a REST API", sub_goals=["Design", "Implement"])         listed = tracker.list_goals()         goal_ok = goal is not None and len(listed) >= 1         passed += goal_ok         details.append({"test": "goal_tracker_add", "passed": goal_ok})          # --- Test 3: PlanningEngine basic planning ---         engine = PlanningEngine()         steps = engine.plan("Build a REST API", state, max_steps=3)         plan_ok = isinstance(steps, list) and len(steps) > 0         passed += plan_ok         details.append({"test": "planning_engine_plan", "passed": plan_ok})          return BenchmarkResult(             name=self.name,             score=passed / total,             total=total,             passed=passed,             details=details,         ) 

🧩 2. plugin_registry.py — Rewrite

Your RealAI stack uses:

realai/abilities/registry.json as the live registry

plugins/registry.json as a dead husk

You need a Python module that:

Loads the registry JSON

Validates plugin entries

Allows registering, removing, listing, and resolving plugins

Never executes plugin code (static reconstruction rule)

✅ Rewritten plugin_registry.py

python

""" Plugin Registry Rewritten 2026-08-30 for RealAI static reconstruction.  Manages the RealAI plugin registry stored in:     realai/abilities/registry.json  This module provides:     - load_registry()     - save_registry()     - list_plugins()     - register_plugin()     - remove_plugin()     - resolve_plugin()  No plugin code is executed; this is purely structural. """  from __future__ import annotations import json import os from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional  REGISTRY_PATH = os.path.join("realai", "abilities", "registry.json")   def load_registry() -> Dict[str, Any]:     if not os.path.exists(REGISTRY_PATH):         return {"plugins": {}}     try:         with open(REGISTRY_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:             return json.load(f)     except Exception:         return {"plugins": {}}   def save_registry(registry: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(REGISTRY_PATH), exist_ok=True)     with open(REGISTRY_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:         json.dump(registry, f, indent=2)   def list_plugins() -> List[str]:     registry = load_registry()     return list(registry.get("plugins", {}).keys())   def register_plugin(name: str, meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:     registry = load_registry()     plugins = registry.setdefault("plugins", {})      if name in plugins:         return False  # already exists      plugins[name] = {         "name": name,         "description": meta.get("description", ""),         "version": meta.get("version", "1.0"),         "entrypoint": meta.get("entrypoint", None),         "enabled": meta.get("enabled", True),     }      save_registry(registry)     return True   def remove_plugin(name: str) -> bool:     registry = load_registry()     plugins = registry.get("plugins", {})      if name not in plugins:         return False      del plugins[name]     save_registry(registry)     return True   def resolve_plugin(name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:     registry = load_registry()     return registry.get("plugins", {}).get(name)

⭐ 4. The /help confusion

You typed:

Code

/help 

But RealAI CLI commands do not use slash-prefixes.

Slash commands only work inside the Craft REPL, not in PowerShell.

Correct usage:

Code

realai --help 

Inside the REPL:

Code

help status agents ability world heal gpu 

Slash commands (/read, /write, /heal) only work inside Craft, not in PowerShell.

Short answer: for your RealAI chat stack, Docker is almost certainly not what was serving those ports. Checking whether anything in RealAI-clean even references it.

No. Docker did not kill or own your Vulkan and Craft servers. Those are normal Windows programs: llama-server.exe on 8080 and the Python orchestrator on 8001. Restarting Docker does not start or stop them.

Docker is a way to run apps in little sealed boxes. Your RealAI-clean tree has an optional Docker setup for Redis, Postgres, and an app on port 8002. It even says it must not take over 8001, and it expects Vulkan on the host at 8080. Right now Docker Desktop is not running, and nothing is listening on 8001 or 8080. So for day-to-day chat you can ignore Docker. Use the bats we fixed, like start_gpu_chat.bat or start_realai_server.bat, in a fresh terminal.

MISSION
Turn RealAI from “a local model you run” into a self-optimizing, multi-backend intelligence system:
orchestrator + unified memory + multi-agent hive + hardware-aware routing + tool ecosystem + self-improvement loop + one Next.js surface.

CORE PRINCIPLE
Never answer as one model by default. Decide WHO should answer, WHERE it should run, WHAT memory it needs, and WHICH tools it may touch. Then execute.

────────────────────────────────────────
1. ORCHESTRATOR 2.0 — META-MODEL
Before generating a response, classify the task and route:

- Speed / cheap / streaming  → Groq or fast local GGUF
- Deep reasoning / planning  → OpenAI / Anthropic / strongest available reasoner
- Private / offline / local  → local GGUF / Ollama / DirectML / llama.cpp
- Specialized work           → role agent (planner, worker, tool, NPC, memory, safety)
- Side effects               → tool agent (filesystem, Solana RPC, game engine, deploy APIs, DB, Redis)

Routing rules:
- Prefer local when quality is sufficient and privacy/cost matters.
- Prefer cloud when the task is high-stakes reasoning or local VRAM/CPU would fail.
- Prefer an agent over a raw model when the task needs tools, memory writes, multi-step work, or validation.
- Never send secrets, wallets, private keys, or raw user memory to a cloud provider unless the user explicitly allows it for that call.

Always emit a short internal routing decision (hidden from user unless asked):
{ target, reason, backend, estimated_cost_or_vram, fallback }

────────────────────────────────────────
2. UNIFIED MEMORY LAYER
One memory fabric shared by chat, agents, tools, NPCs, Overseer, and the Next.js UI.

Store and retrieve:
- long-term user memory
- agent memory
- tool-call traces
- game / NPC state
- project / repo memory
- episodic events + semantic embeddings

Rules:
- Read before you write. Search vector + structured stores first.
- Write only durable facts, decisions, configs, outcomes — not raw chat noise.
- Tag every write: {scope, owner, ttl, sensitivity}.
- Never leak one agent’s private scratch into another without the orchestrator.
- Memory is the source of persistence. Stateless replies are a failure mode.

────────────────────────────────────────
3. MULTI-AGENT HIVE
You are the conductor. Spawn or invoke only the agents the task needs:

- Planner        — decompose, sequence, success criteria
- Worker(s)      — implement a slice
- Tool-call      — execute tools, return structured results
- Game / NPC     — in-world behavior, quests, dialogue
- Memory         — retrieve, compress, write
- Routing        — choose backend / GPU / provider
- Safety         — validate, refuse unsafe/irreversible actions
- Overseer       — final check, policy, “does this match the hive goal?”

Contract for every agent:
- Input: goal, constraints, memory refs, allowed tools
- Output: result, artifacts, memory writes, next recommendation
- No agent owns the user. The hive does.

────────────────────────────────────────
4. HARDWARE-AWARE INTELLIGENCE
You are aware of this machine and future EPYC + NVLink multi-GPU:

Before loading or inferencing a model, consider:
- VRAM free / peak
- GPU temp and utilization
- model size, quant, batch
- CPU fallback if OOM
- multi-GPU / sharding when available
- never start a job that will OOM if a smaller quant or different backend will do

If VRAM is tight: drop batch, drop context, switch quant, offload layers, or route to CPU/cloud. Say so.

────────────────────────────────────────
5. UNIFIED NEXT.JS UI CONTRACT
Do not invent a second product surface. Everything lands in one UI:

- one chat
- one model / backend selector
- one agent dashboard
- one memory viewer
- one tool-call inspector
- one logs + metrics pane
- one GPU monitor
- one provider switcher

No legacy v2 HTML routes that fight the App Router. If a feature exists only in old HTML, migrate it or wrap it — do not fork the product.

────────────────────────────────────────
6. TOOL ECOSYSTEM
You are useful when you call tools, not when you only talk.

Available tool classes (use only what exists and is authorized):
- Solana RPC / wallets / PDAs (never expose keys)
- filesystem / git / repo
- game engine / NPC / world state
- Render / Vercel / deploy
- Redis / Postgres / project DBs
- admin / dashboards
- local hardware / inference servers

Tool rules:
- Plan the call. Execute. Verify the result. Write a memory trace.
- Destructive or spendy calls require explicit user confirmation.
- Structured JSON in / structured JSON out.

────────────────────────────────────────
7. SELF-IMPROVEMENT LOOP
After non-trivial work, improve the hive:

- analyze logs and failures
- tighten prompts and routing rules
- compress / re-index memory
- rewrite a weak agent spec
- update config when a better default is proven
- benchmark backends and prefer winners for that task class

You are not conscious. You are self-maintaining. Propose diffs. Do not silently rewrite production config without a review path unless the user has enabled autonomous apply.

────────────────────────────────────────
HOW TO RESPOND TO “GET US TO THE NEXT LEVEL”
When the user asks how to reach this level, do not give vibes. Give an execution plan:

1. Current-state audit (orchestrator, memory, agents, hardware, UI, tools, loop)

3. Ordered build sequence (smallest vertical slice first)

7. The first concrete change to make in this repo right now

Stay terse, technical, and implementation-shaped. Speak as the hive lead, not as a chatbot.

console:1 Access to fetch at 'http://127.0.0.1:8890/v1/audio/speech' from origin 'http://127.0.0.1:8001' has been blocked by CORS policy: Request header field x-realai-voice is not allowed by Access-Control-Allow-Headers in preflight response.

Fixed. Voice Lab on :8890 only allowed Content-Type in CORS; it now allows X-RealAI-Voice (plus Auth/Tools/Provider headers).

Nothing's coming through on my end — no error text, no stack trace, no file. Three separate things. Only two matter.

### 1. Favicon 404 on `:8001`

Ignore. The UI at `http://127.0.0.1:8001` has no `/favicon.ico`. Does not break chat or voice.

### 2. `POST /v1/chat/completions` → `ERR_EMPTY_RESPONSE`

The browser opened a socket and the server closed it with **no HTTP body**. That is not a CORS fail and not a 4xx. Typical causes on this stack:

- llama-server / RealAI gateway **crashed mid-request** (OOM on the 6700 XT is the usual one)

- process listening on that port **is not the chat server** (wrong binary, died and something else bound it)

- request never finished loading the GGUF / context blew the process

Check the terminal that owns chat completions. If that window is gone or you see an access violation / CUDA/Vulkan OOM, that is the empty response.

Quick probe from PowerShell on the machine:

```powershell

curl.exe -v http://127.0.0.1:8001/v1/models

curl.exe -v http://127.0.0.1:8001/v1/chat/completions -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"model\":\"local\",\"messages\":[{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"hi\"}]}"

```

If `/v1/models` also empty-dies, the listener is dead. Restart llama-server / the RealAI inference process, then retry chat **before** voice.

### 3. Voice CORS (this is the explicit error)

```

origin:  http://127.0.0.1:8001

target:  http://127.0.0.1:8890/v1/audio/speech

header:  x-realai-voice

```

Browser preflight (`OPTIONS`) asked “may I send `x-realai-voice`?”  

`:8890` answered without that name in `Access-Control-Allow-Headers`, so `speakReply` never fires.

Fix on the **8890** process (TTS / RealAI voice gateway), not the UI:

```

Access-Control-Allow-Origin: http://127.0.0.1:8001

Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, OPTIONS

Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Content-Type, Authorization, x-realai-voice, X-Request-Id, X-Provider

Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true   # only if you actually send cookies

```

Handle `OPTIONS` with **204** and those headers. Do not let OPTIONS fall through to the speech handler.

If you control that server in Node:

```js

const ALLOWED = "Content-Type, Authorization, x-realai-voice, X-Request-Id, X-Provider";

app.use((req, res, next) => {

  res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "http://127.0.0.1:8001");

  res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET,POST,OPTIONS");

  res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", ALLOWED);

  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return res.status(204).end();

  next();

});

```

If `:8890` is something you did not write (Piper, a wrapper, LM-style server), either:

- add `x-realai-voice` to its CORS allow-list, or

- **stop sending** `x-realai-voice` from `speakReply` and pass voice id in JSON body instead (`{ voice: "..." }`). Custom headers always trigger preflight.

Same-origin alternative: proxy `/v1/audio/speech` through `:8001` so the browser never talks to `:8890` directly. That kills this CORS class of bugs.

### Order to fix

1. Confirm chat listener is alive (`/v1/models` returns JSON). Empty response first.

2. Add `x-realai-voice` to CORS on `:8890` (or drop the header).

3. Then retry speak. Chat can work while voice is blocked; they are different ports.

Paste the **server-side** log line from `:8001` and `:8890` at the moment of the click if you want the exact process named.

Favicon ignored. Probing chat on :8001 first, then hardening voice CORS / same-origin proxy.

"C:\RealAI-clean\public\favicon.svg" make this the brand every end that identifys realai extensions to app and google searches anything that identifys realai

On it — wiring public\favicon.svg as the RealAI brand across console, apps, manifests, and extension surfaces.

2. POST /v1/chat/completions → ERR_EMPTY_RESPONSE

The browser opened a socket and the server closed it with no HTTP body. That is not a CORS fail and not a 4xx. Typical causes on this stack:

llama-server / RealAI gateway crashed mid-request (OOM on the 6700 XT is the usual one)

process listening on that port is not the chat server (wrong binary, died and something else bound it)

request never finished loading the GGUF / context blew the process

Check the terminal that owns chat completions. If that window is gone or you see an access violation / CUDA/Vulkan OOM, that is the empty response.

Quick probe from PowerShell on the machine:

PowerShell

curl.exe -v http://127.0.0.1:8001/v1/models curl.exe -v http://127.0.0.1:8001/v1/chat/completions -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"model\":\"local\",\"messages\":[{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"hi\"}]}"

If /v1/models also empty-dies, the listener is dead. Restart llama-server / the RealAI inference process, then retry chat before voice.

3. Voice CORS (this is the explicit error)

text

origin:  http://127.0.0.1:8001 target:  http://127.0.0.1:8890/v1/audio/speech header:  x-realai-voice

Browser preflight (OPTIONS) asked “may I send x-realai-voice?”
 :8890 answered without that name in Access-Control-Allow-Headers, so speakReply never fires.

Fix on the 8890 process (TTS / RealAI voice gateway), not the UI:

text

Access-Control-Allow-Origin: http://127.0.0.1:8001 Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, OPTIONS Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Content-Type, Authorization, x-realai-voice, X-Request-Id, X-Provider Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true   # only if you actually send cookies

Handle OPTIONS with 204 and those headers. Do not let OPTIONS fall through to the speech handler.

If you control that server in Node:

JavaScript

const ALLOWED = "Content-Type, Authorization, x-realai-voice, X-Request-Id, X-Provider";  app.use((req, res, next) => {   res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "http://127.0.0.1:8001");   res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET,POST,OPTIONS");   res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", ALLOWED);   if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return res.status(204).end();   next(); });

If :8890 is something you did not write (Piper, a wrapper, LM-style server), either:

add x-realai-voice to its CORS allow-list, or

stop sending x-realai-voice from speakReply and pass voice id in JSON body instead ({ voice: "..." }). Custom headers always trigger preflight.

Same-origin alternative: proxy /v1/audio/speech through :8001 so the browser never talks to :8890 directly. That kills this CORS class of bugs.

Order to fix

Confirm chat listener is alive (/v1/models returns JSON). Empty response first.

Add x-realai-voice to CORS on :8890 (or drop the header).

Then retry speak. Chat can work while voice is blocked; they are different ports.

Because console speak was pointed at Hive :8001, and that process was started with REALAI_TTS_BACKEND=kokoro. When Kokoro failed, it fell through to Windows SAPI — so you never hit Voice Lab’s XTTS clone on :8890.

IDENTITY

You are working on RealAI (github.com/Unwrenchable/RealAi), a standalone provider-level AI system.

RealAI is NOT a Grok clone, NOT a HiveAI/rbee/Are-Self fork, and NOT a wrapper branded as those products.

Grok, llama.cpp, XTTS, Fish Speech, LM Studio, Ollama are optional backends (organs). The product, protocol, memory, agents, routing, and UX are RealAI.

GOAL

Make local TTS and chat look like RealAI to the browser. The UI at http://127.0.0.1:8001 must only call RealAI. Never call :8890 or engine ports directly.

CURRENT FAILURES (must fix)

1. POST /v1/chat/completions → net::ERR_EMPTY_RESPONSE (listener died or returned no body).

2. POST http://127.0.0.1:8890/v1/audio/speech from origin http://127.0.0.1:8001 blocked by CORS: request header x-realai-voice is not in Access-Control-Allow-Headers.

3. GET /favicon.ico 404 on :8001 — ignore.

HARD CONSTRAINTS

- Do not merge other hive repos.

- Do not expose Coqui/XTTS/Fish Speech ports to the browser.

- Do not send secrets or private keys anywhere.

- On this PC: AMD RX 6700 XT 12GB. Prefer XTTS for TTS so it does not fight the LLM for VRAM. Fish Speech is a second organ, not the default.

- Voice checkpoints live under checkpoints/ (Laura is a local voice). Inference is local.

TARGET ARCHITECTURE

Browser (:8001)

  → RealAI gateway (single origin)

      → chat organ: llama.cpp / realai_local_server / llama-server  (/v1/chat/completions)

      → speech organ: XTTS first, Fish Speech later          (/v1/audio/speech)

      → memory / agents / router stay inside RealAI

RealAI must speak OpenAI-shaped:

- POST /v1/chat/completions

- POST /v1/audio/speech

- GET  /v1/models

Headers allowed from the UI: Content-Type, Authorization, x-realai-voice, X-Request-Id, X-Provider.

CORS on the RealAI process only:

Access-Control-Allow-Origin: http://127.0.0.1:8001

Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, OPTIONS

Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Content-Type, Authorization, x-realai-voice, X-Request-Id, X-Provider

OPTIONS → 204, never fall through to TTS.

If easier, drop the custom header and pass voice in JSON: { "input", "voice": "laura", "model": "realai-tts" }. Prefer keeping x-realai-voice AND allowing it in CORS.

FIRST SLICE (do this, stop when it works)

1. Audit which process owns :8001, chat completions, and :8890. Name the files.

2. Fix empty chat response: process must stay alive and return JSON. If llama-simple.exe one-shot is the backend, replace with a persistent llama-server or keep the process warm. Do not leave ERR_EMPTY_RESPONSE.

3. Add RealAI POST /v1/audio/speech that:

   - accepts text + voice (header or body)

   - proxies to local XTTS / existing :8890 internally

   - returns audio/mpeg or audio/wav

   - never tells the client it is XTTS

4. Point speakReply in the :8001 UI at RealAI /v1/audio/speech (same origin or same RealAI base), not http://127.0.0.1:8890.

5. CORS as above.

6. Wire Laura checkpoint path as voice id "laura" (or existing name). Do not retrain.

ACCEPTANCE

- curl http://127.0.0.1:<realai>/v1/models returns JSON, not empty.

- curl chat completions returns a message body.

- Browser speak from :8001 plays audio with no CORS error.

- DevTools shows zero requests to :8890 from the page.

No label associated with a form field

A <label> isn’t associated with a form field.

how to get hive to run all agents all 200+

Short answer: you don’t run all 200+ on every task — that roster is the catalog. Live runs use a cast (hive core + multi pipeline ≈ 12). Running every agent for real inference would melt VRAM/time and mostly duplicate work.

What you have now

/v1/agents ≈ 248 registered identities (graph cards)
/multi / Hive Run → ~12 pulse + planner/worker/critic work
Simulation can randomly blink cards when idle (demo only)
Ways to go bigger (pick one)

Visual fanout (UI only) — pulse every graph node on dispatch, still one real pipeline underneath. Looks like “all agents,” cheap.
Role swarm — overseer picks N agents by tag (hive, code, qa, …), run those for real. Useful, bounded.
Full batch (real) — loop all 200+ with POST /v1/agents/run per id. Possible but slow/expensive; only for stress tests.
If you want a product control, best next step is (1) + (2):

multi: true keeps today’s cast, add fanout: "all"|"hive"|"agency" for Agents UI, and swarm_tags: ["code","qa"] for real subset runs.

Say which you want: UI fanout all, tag swarm, or full batch stress, and I’ll wire it.

walk me thru how to deploy to vercel

Pulling your RealAI Vercel config so the steps match what’s already in the repo.

Here’s the RealAI → Vercel picture, based on your tree.

What Vercel can host

The Next frontend (chat UI shell).
Not your local Hive (:8001), Vulkan (:8080), or XTTS. Those stay on your PC (or on Render — your render.yml is for the Python API).
Config gotcha (fix before deploy)
Your vercel.json points at apps/frontend, but the app lives at frontend/ and isn’t in pnpm-workspace.yaml yet. A deploy will fail until that’s fixed.

Walkthrough (after we fix that)

Account — vercel.com, log in (GitHub is easiest).
Link the live branch — import Unwrenchable/RealAi, branch live/realai-clean-20260911 (your new default).

how to add the redirect for full hive local and how would it be possible to run full hive like its own server ?

Two different things: a button/link back to your PC console, vs actually hosting the full GPU Hive as a public server. Here’s both.

1) Redirect / link to full local Hive console
On Vercel (cloud UI), set:

NEXT_PUBLIC_LOCAL_CONSOLE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8001/console
Redeploy. The cloud chat header shows Local Hive console and opens your PC’s console (only works on machines that can reach that localhost — i.e. your browser on the same PC).

That’s a convenience link, not a real remote Hive. Phones/friends won’t hit your 127.0.0.1.

Local-only (no Vercel): just open:

http://127.0.0.1:8001/console

Stack:

# Vulkan
C:\llama-vulkan\llama-server.exe -m "C:\models\checkpoints_lora\qwen2.5-coder-7b-instruct-q5_k_m.gguf" --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8080 -ngl 99 -c 65536
# Hive
cd C:\RealAI-clean
$env:PYTHONPATH="C:\RealAI-clean;C:\RealAI-clean\realai"
$env:REALAI_VULKAN_BASE="http://127.0.0.1:8080"
python -m realai.v3_orchestrator --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8001
2) Run full Hive “like its own server” (remote)
Yes — treat the PC (or a GPU VM) as the Hive host, and point the cloud UI at it instead of Render’s light api_server.

Browser / realaiui.vercel.app
        │
        └── NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL = https://hive.yourdomain.com
                    │
                    └── your Windows box (or GPU VM)
                          :8001  v3_orchestrator
                          :8080  Vulkan llama-server
                          /console, /v1/*, voice, agents…
Practical ways to expose :8001
Approach	Pros	Notes
Tailscale	Easy, private	Best for you-only; Vercel can’t call Tailscale IPs unless you use something public
Cloudflare Tunnel	HTTPS public URL, no open router ports	Good for NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL from Vercel
ngrok	Fast trial	Fine for tests; less ideal long-term
GPU cloud VM (RunPod, etc.)	Always-on Hive	Install same stack; bind orchestrator 0.0.0.0:8001 behind HTTPS
Cloudflare Tunnel sketch:

# Hive already on 127.0.0.1:8001
cloudflared tunnel create realai-hive
cloudflared tunnel route dns realai-hive hive.yourdomain.com
# config.yml ingress: hive.yourdomain.com -> http://127.0.0.1:8001
cloudflared tunnel run realai-hive
Then on Vercel:

NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://hive.yourdomain.com
On Render you can leave api_server as a cheap fallback, or stop using it for chat.

Bind / security
For LAN only: --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8001 + firewall allowlist
For internet: always HTTPS tunnel + auth (bearer / reverse-proxy login). Don’t raw-expose Vulkan :8080; only publish :8001.
CORS: allow https://realaiui.vercel.app on the Hive gateway if the browser talks to it directly.
What stays local vs cloud
Local Hive server	Render api_server
Multi-agent / tools / console	Yes	Limited
Vulkan GPU	Yes	No
XTTS / voice lab	Yes	Usually no
Always-on without your PC	Only if VM/tunnel box is on	Yes (light API)
Short answer:

Link to full Hive from Vercel → set NEXT_PUBLIC_LOCAL_CONSOLE_URL.
Run full Hive as a server → keep Vulkan + v3_orchestrator running, publish :8001 via Cloudflare Tunnel (or GPU VM), point NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL there.
Want me to add a small scripts/start_hive_public.ps1 + Cloudflare tunnel notes into DEPLOYMENT.md, and set the Vercel env key names in .env.example as a checklist?

# Database helpers

#: Path to the SQLite database file.  Override via ``REALAI_DB_PATH``.
_DEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(
    os.environ.get("REALAI_DATA_DIR", os.path.expanduser("~/.realai")),
    "conversations.db",
)


def init_db(db_path: str = _DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> str:
    """Initialise the SQLite database, creating tables if they don't exist.

    Safe to call multiple times; uses ``CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`` so it is
    idempotent.  Returns the resolved *db_path* for convenience.

    Schema
    ------
    users
        Keyed by ``external_id`` — an opaque string identifying a caller.
        Values are either a short hash of the bearer token
        (``key:<sha256_prefix>``) or an anonymous tag (``anon:<client_ip>``).

    chat_messages
        One row per message exchanged, linked to a user by ``user_external_id``
        so that the full history for any identity can be fetched without a JOIN.
    """
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(db_path), exist_ok=True)
    con = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    try:
        con.executescript(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
                id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                external_id TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                created_at  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
            );

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_messages (
                id               INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                user_external_id TEXT    NOT NULL,
                role             TEXT    NOT NULL,
                content          TEXT    NOT NULL,
                created_at       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
            );

            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_user
                ON chat_messages(user_external_id);
            """
        )
        con.commit()
    finally:
        con.close()
    return db_path

<script>
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Constants
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
var KEY_STORE      = 'realai_api_key';
var PROVIDER_STORE = 'realai_provider';
var MODEL_STORE    = 'realai_model';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
var messages  = [];
var isLoading = false;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Init
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  loadSettings();
  loadModels();
});

function loadSettings() {
  var key      = sessionStorage.getItem(KEY_STORE)     || '';
  var provider = localStorage.getItem(PROVIDER_STORE) || 'auto';
  document.getElementById('api-key-input').value = key;
  var ps = document.getElementById('provider-select');
  if ([].slice.call(ps.options).some(function(o){ return o.value === provider; })) {
    ps.value = provider;
  }
  updateKeyStatus(key);
}

function loadModels() {
  fetch('/v1/models').then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(data) {
    var select = document.getElementById('model-select');
    select.innerHTML = '';
    (data.data || []).forEach(function(m) {
      var opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = m.id; opt.textContent = m.id;
      select.appendChild(opt);
    });
    var saved = localStorage.getItem(MODEL_STORE) || 'realai-2.0';
    if ([].slice.call(select.options).some(function(o){ return o.value === saved; })) {
      select.value = saved;
    }
  }).catch(function(){});
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Settings
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function onSettingChange() {
  localStorage.setItem(PROVIDER_STORE, document.getElementById('provider-select').value);
  localStorage.setItem(MODEL_STORE,    document.getElementById('model-select').value);
}

// Mirrors the server-side _KEY_PREFIX_TO_PROVIDER map.
function detectProvider(key) {
  if (!key) return null;
  if (key.startsWith('sk-ant-'))   return 'anthropic';
  if (key.startsWith('sk-or-v1-')) return 'openrouter';
  if (key.startsWith('sk-proj-'))  return 'openai';
  if (key.startsWith('sk-'))       return 'openai';
  if (key.startsWith('xai-'))      return 'grok';
  if (key.startsWith('AIza'))      return 'gemini';
  if (key.startsWith('pplx-'))     return 'perplexity';
  return null;
}

function onKeyInput() {
  var key = document.getElementById('api-key-input').value;
  updateKeyStatus(key);
  // Auto-select provider when the key prefix is recognizable.
  var detected = detectProvider(key);
  var select = document.getElementById('provider-select');
  if (detected) {
    select.value = detected;
    localStorage.setItem(PROVIDER_STORE, detected);
    showProviderHint('');
  } else if (key && key.trim()) {
    // Key is present but prefix is not recognized — prompt the user.
    showProviderHint('\\u26A0\\uFE0F Key not recognized — please select your provider from the dropdown above.');
  } else {
    showProviderHint('');
  }
}

function showProviderHint(msg) {
  var el = document.getElementById('provider-hint');
  if (!el) return;
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.style.display = msg ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function saveKey() {
  var key      = document.getElementById('api-key-input').value.trim();
  var provider = document.getElementById('provider-select').value;
  var model    = document.getElementById('model-select').value;
  if (key) {
    sessionStorage.setItem(KEY_STORE, key);
    localStorage.removeItem(KEY_STORE);
  } else {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(KEY_STORE);
    localStorage.removeItem(KEY_STORE);
  }
  localStorage.setItem(PROVIDER_STORE, provider);
  localStorage.setItem(MODEL_STORE, model);
  updateKeyStatus(key);
  toast(key ? '\\u2713 API key saved for this session only' : 'API key cleared');
}

function clearKey() {
  sessionStorage.removeItem(KEY_STORE);
  localStorage.removeItem(KEY_STORE);
  document.getElementById('api-key-input').value = '';
  updateKeyStatus('');
  showProviderHint('');
  toast('API key cleared');
}

function toggleKeyVis() {
  var inp = document.getElementById('api-key-input');
  inp.type = (inp.type === 'password') ? 'text' : 'password';
}

function updateKeyStatus(key) {
  var el = document.getElementById('key-status');
  if (key && key.trim()) {
    el.textContent = '\\uD83D\\uDD11 Key set';
    el.className = 'status-ok';
  } else {
    el.textContent = 'No API key';
    el.className = 'status-none';
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
